[![](https://github.com/embeddings-benchmark/mteb/raw/main/docs/images/logos/hf_logo.png?raw=true)](https://huggingface.co/spaces/mteb/leaderboard)\n\n[](#----)\n\n## Installation\n\n[](#installation)\n\nYou can install mteb simply using pip or uv. For more on installation please see the [documentation](https://embeddings-benchmark.github.io/mteb/installation/).\n\npip install mteb\n\nFor faster installation, you can also use [uv](https://docs.astral.sh/uv/):\n\nuv add mteb\n\n## Example Usage\n\n[](#example-usage)\n\nBelow we present a simple use-case example. For more information, see the [documentation](https://embeddings-benchmark.github.io/mteb/).\n\nimport mteb\nfrom sentence\\_transformers import SentenceTransformer\n\n\\# Select model\nmodel\\_name \\= \"sentence-transformers/all-MiniLM-L6-v2\"\nmodel \\= mteb.get\\_model(model\\_name) \\# if the model is not implemented in MTEB it will be eq. to SentenceTransformer(model\\_name)\n\n\\# Select tasks\ntasks \\= mteb.get\\_tasks(tasks\\=\\[\"Banking77Classification.v2\"\\])\n\n\\# evaluate\nresults \\= mteb.evaluate(model, tasks\\=tasks)\n\nYou can also run it using the CLI:\n\nmteb run \\\\\n    -m sentence-transformers/all-MiniLM-L6-v2 \\\\\n    -t \"Banking77Classification.v2\" \\\\\n    --output-folder results\n\nFor more on how to use the CLI check out the [related documentation](https://embeddings-benchmark.github.io/mteb/get_started/usage/cli/).\n\n## Overview\n\n[](#overview)\n\nOverview\n\n📈
